# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-26)

## Corpus Check
- 5568 files · ~13,036,060 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 293 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .log 176, (none) 34, .css 16)

## Summary
- 69696 nodes · 206282 edges · 1390 communities (1261 shown, 129 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8576 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `64a53cba`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
